# 咨询入口接入完成报告

日期：2026-10-07T23:02:42.743372+08:00
当前项：第三项“接入最终咨询入口”。15/15类已写入并完成执行方内容核对、引用/原文保护与必要工程检查，剩余0类；正式提交本项独立验收。执行完成不替代监督验收，不自动进入第四项。

本项沿用前两项已验收的工作流与条件版本，给已有模块补需求选择、具体资源、交付与状态交接。保留原有15类框架、61库和51来源、38份历史稿；没有重新扩写语言库、制作示范作品或安排15类创作演练。

授权与依赖：[当前授权](%E5%BD%93%E5%89%8D%E6%8E%88%E6%9D%83%E8%8C%83%E5%9B%B4.json)；[顺序执行约定](%E5%9B%9B%E9%A1%B9%E6%94%B6%E5%8F%A3_%E9%A1%BA%E5%BA%8F%E6%89%A7%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%8E%E9%AA%8C%E6%94%B6.md)；[第一项验收](%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E8%A1%A5%E9%BD%90_%E7%8B%AC%E7%AB%8B%E9%AA%8C%E6%94%B6%E6%8A%A5%E5%91%8A.md)；[第二项验收](%E7%89%88%E6%9C%AC%E6%9C%89%E6%95%88%E5%86%85%E5%AE%B9%E6%95%B4%E5%90%88_%E7%8B%AC%E7%AB%8B%E9%AA%8C%E6%94%B6%E6%8A%A5%E5%91%8A.md)。

## 实际改动与读取

完整读取现有15模块、SKILL、consultation、project_state及工作入口的有效正文与导航；按前两项验收记录复用已读原稿与采用证据。必要内容复核包括三维建模/交互分支、剪辑交接、教学起点和H017/H004/H007当前条件层。未重新阅读和独立语义复审全部3876词条。

模块新增节保留原正文为完整前缀。`Modes`索引新增CurrentWorkflowChoices/ConditionalWorkflowChoices/AuxiliaryDeepChoices，记录条件、ID、位置及交接；保留并补齐旧工作流/库引用。检索脚本route/catalog返回稳定ID摘要，不重复每个资源的完整路径与交接；read读取完整模块，deep读取唯一完整段落。既有域路由测试加强了摘要定位及正文完整读取检查。SKILL现有调用已能抵达入口与共同规则，本项未修改SKILL。

以下精确资源链接带当前MD行号；SourceId是独立来源，HistoryId是历史版本，SRC/L任务ID是实际深度段落。每类按触发条件读取，不自动执行全部候选。

### 创作策划与想法分析（strategy）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/01_创作策划与想法分析.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/01_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E7%AD%96%E5%88%92%E4%B8%8E%E6%83%B3%E6%B3%95%E5%88%86%E6%9E%90.md#L44)。
- 选择问题：只有想法、主题或用途，想判断做什么、为什么值得做、先走哪条创作路线。

**当前主流程**

- 故事或短剧概念比较：[SRC048-S001｜完整输入与类型、命名、输出要求的采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L324)；[SRC025-S001｜需求、模式和范围判断](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E4%BC%98%E8%B4%A8%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L229)。只取需求、类型和范围判断；尚在分析时不自动写正文。
- 选择题材及其因果组合：[SRC044-S001｜十六题材目录的实际作用与交叉关系](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E9%A2%98%E6%9D%90%E5%8F%82%E8%80%83_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L171)；[SRC044-S002｜可用的选题比较、推荐和完整复用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E9%A2%98%E6%9D%90%E5%8F%82%E8%80%83_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L194)。优先比较已有题材；类型标签不能代替事件、人物目标与代价。

- [H023](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E5%9C%BA%E9%9D%A2%E8%B0%83%E5%BA%A6%E5%BA%93_%E5%8F%99%E4%BA%8B%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%BA%93_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC021-S003｜H023双库二十调度六十叙事方法的条件整合](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%8F%99%E4%BA%8B%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L509)：结构与场面调度共同影响创意时；宏观叙事时段不代替单镜时间。

**必要辅助候选**

- [L18-T0001｜三幕式结构（Three-Act Structure）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/18_%E5%8F%99%E4%BA%8B%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L442)：比较三幕信息分配
- [L48-T0001｜成长弧光](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/48_%E8%A7%92%E8%89%B2%E5%BC%A7%E5%85%89%E4%B8%8E%E5%85%B3%E7%B3%BB%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L443)：核对人物成长的行动后果
- [L49-T0001｜第一人称](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/49_%E5%8F%99%E4%BA%8B%E8%A7%86%E8%A7%92%E4%B8%8E%E6%97%B6%E9%97%B4%E7%A9%BA%E9%97%B4%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L444)：评估第一人称的信息限制

- 具体交付：分析/比较交付一句话承诺、推荐方向及必要备选、依据、制作条件和关键待选项；授权制作后才交指定大纲/场次等成果。
- 上游输入：接收想法/素材版本、目标受众、用户已确定方向和本次授权范围；无需先补齐整剧资产。
- 下游交接：向outline/screenplay/images/music中本次所选模块传目标、主题、主类型/辅类型、锁定人物事实、已选风格、待选事项及授权交付；其余候选不自动执行。

### 小说、题材与大纲（outline）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/02_小说、题材与大纲.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/02_%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E3%80%81%E9%A2%98%E6%9D%90%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2.md#L43)。
- 选择问题：需要选小说题材、设计人物关系与世界规则、写大纲/章节/正文或继续已有小说。

**当前主流程**

- 题材筛选或混合类型：[SRC044-S001｜十六题材目录的实际作用与交叉关系](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E9%A2%98%E6%9D%90%E5%8F%82%E8%80%83_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L171)；[SRC044-S002｜可用的选题比较、推荐和完整复用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E9%A2%98%E6%9D%90%E5%8F%82%E8%80%83_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L194)。先比较主次与因果，已有选题不重新要求确认。
- 小说规划、正文或续写：[SRC048-S001｜完整输入与类型、命名、输出要求的采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L324)；[SRC048-S002｜书名、两层简介和完整一章大纲](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L332)；[SRC048-S003｜一章完整正文与原数值要求](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L350)；[SRC048-S004｜具体自审、原规则冲突与接续](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L588)。按用户指定书名/简介/大纲/正文范围调用；来源固定字数与当前目标冲突时明示，不悄悄删要求。

**条件历史版**

- [H030](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E9%A2%98%E6%9D%90%E5%8F%82%E8%80%83_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC044-S003｜H030题材卡、融合因果与篇幅预算的条件采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E9%A2%98%E6%9D%90%E5%8F%82%E8%80%83_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L200)：需要14字段题材卡、16种融合方法或分阶段篇幅预算；不把推荐预算写成用户确认。
- [H023](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E5%9C%BA%E9%9D%A2%E8%B0%83%E5%BA%A6%E5%BA%93_%E5%8F%99%E4%BA%8B%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%BA%93_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC021-S003｜H023双库二十调度六十叙事方法的条件整合](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%8F%99%E4%BA%8B%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L509)：需要对照结构与信息释放时；事件发生时间、叙述顺序和角色知识分别记录。

**必要辅助候选**

- [L18-T0001｜三幕式结构（Three-Act Structure）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/18_%E5%8F%99%E4%BA%8B%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L442)：三幕结构候选
- [L48-T0001｜成长弧光](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/48_%E8%A7%92%E8%89%B2%E5%BC%A7%E5%85%89%E4%B8%8E%E5%85%B3%E7%B3%BB%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L443)：成长弧光与行为证据
- [L27-T0001｜信息传递型对话（Information Delivery Dialogue）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/27_%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%B0%E8%AF%8D%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L503)：对白承载必要信息

- 具体交付：咨询交付选题/结构建议；授权后交指定大纲、细纲或完整正文，以及对应原句/章节覆盖和续写断点。
- 上游输入：继承strategy已选方向、原文版本、人物/世界事实、允许新增范围、已写章节和最后有效状态。
- 下游交接：给screenplay或storyboard传当前有效原稿、章/段/句编号、原句锁、人物关系、知识揭示、道具归属及下一未覆盖句；原著续写与影视改编的新增分别登记。

### 影视剧本与改编（screenplay）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/03_影视剧本与改编.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/03_%E5%BD%B1%E8%A7%86%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E6%94%B9%E7%BC%96.md#L43)。
- 选择问题：将原文改为影视剧本、创作新剧本、补指定场次，或修改/继续现有剧本。

**当前主流程**

- brary/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/GPT%E6%95%85%E4%BA%8B%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E8%BD%AC%E5%BD%B1%E8%A7%86%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L2544)。采用原著A轨与改编B轨、事实等级/权限、场次账本；只交用户要求的编剧/制作/导演层。

%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E4%BC%98%E8%B4%A8%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L285)。大纲、人物、场次、正式稿、自审交接完整，但按本次范围展开。

**条件历史版**

- [H029](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E8%BD%AC%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC050-S006｜H029逐句忠实版的心理信息与交接采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/GPT%E6%95%85%E4%BA%8B%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E8%BD%AC%E5%BD%B1%E8%A7%86%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L2552)：要求逐句忠实且不删任何原句时，采用四步骤与正文派生的对白/音效清单；心理判断对象与未知真假不能只剩表情。
- [H027](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%94%B9%E5%BD%B1%E8%A7%86%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC050-S007｜H027四十六模块双轨、信息权限与三层交付](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/GPT%E6%95%85%E4%BA%8B%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E8%BD%AC%E5%BD%B1%E8%A7%86%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L2562)：需要46模块双轨、信息权限或三层交付时；授权改编与H029逐句保留分开选择。
- [H024](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%B0%E8%AF%8D%E6%8A%80%E5%B7%A7%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC030-S007｜H024七十对白方法与话轮、知识、媒介状态](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/27%E3%80%81%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%B0%E8%AF%8D%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L533)：对白改写/潜台词/话轮工程；忠实模式保原句，沉默不自动代表接受，录完不等于发送或听见。

**必要辅助候选**

- [L27-T0001｜信息传递型对话（Information Delivery Dialogue）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/27_%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%B0%E8%AF%8D%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L503)：对白信息传递
- [L48-T0001｜成长弧光](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/48_%E8%A7%92%E8%89%B2%E5%BC%A7%E5%85%89%E4%B8%8E%E5%85%B3%E7%B3%BB%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L443)：人物行为与变化
- [L49-T0001｜第一人称](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/49_%E5%8F%99%E4%BA%8B%E8%A7%86%E8%A7%92%E4%B8%8E%E6%97%B6%E9%97%B4%E7%A9%BA%E9%97%B4%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L444)：主观信息视角

- 具体交付：咨询交付改编边界和场次安排；授权后交完整指定剧本、原句覆盖、必要制作卡及改动传播清单。
- 上游输入：接原文版本/P-S节点、事实与真假两维、已授权改编权限、角色阶段、场景地图、道具/关系/知识末态。
- 下游交接：给images传C/SC/P实体与资产职责；给storyboard传SC/B/D/V编号、逐字对白、动作起终和声源。旁白让观众知道的内容不自动成为场内角色知识；修改后同步受影响场次。

### 图片、封面与视觉资产（images）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/04_图片、封面与视觉资产.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/04_%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87%E3%80%81%E5%B0%81%E9%9D%A2%E4%B8%8E%E8%A7%86%E8%A7%89%E8%B5%84%E4%BA%A7.md#L53)。
- 选择问题：提取角色/场景/道具资产，做参考套图、封面、三视图、服装板或场景多视角。

**当前主流程**

- 故事实体提取与纯背景/可移动道具分开：[SRC019-S001｜提取输入、分类与版本决策](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/01_%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E6%8F%90%E5%8F%96%E6%95%85%E4%BA%8B%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%AD%E7%9A%84%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E5%9C%BA%E6%99%AF%E9%81%93%E5%85%B7%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L300)；[SRC019-S002｜角色唯一阶段的完整输出](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/01_%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E6%8F%90%E5%8F%96%E6%95%85%E4%BA%8B%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%AD%E7%9A%84%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E5%9C%BA%E6%99%AF%E9%81%93%E5%85%B7%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L308)；[SRC019-S003｜固定场景地图、五视角和排版完整输出](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/01_%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E6%8F%90%E5%8F%96%E6%95%85%E4%BA%8B%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%AD%E7%9A%84%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E5%9C%BA%E6%99%AF%E9%81%93%E5%85%B7%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L324)；[SRC019-S004｜可移动道具的完整输出](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/01_%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E6%8F%90%E5%8F%96%E6%95%85%E4%BA%8B%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%AD%E7%9A%84%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E5%9C%BA%E6%99%AF%E9%81%93%E5%85%B7%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L341)；[SRC019-S005｜实际核对、可调整项与交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/01_%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E6%8F%90%E5%8F%96%E6%95%85%E4%BA%8B%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%AD%E7%9A%84%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E5%9C%BA%E6%99%AF%E9%81%93%E5%85%B7%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L354)。优先V2.7，保持唯一角色阶段、固定地图、可移动道具独立；不把故事人物放进纯场景底图。
- 封面创意及提示词：[SRC035-S001｜九阶段覆盖与剧情提炼、角色依据](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/01_%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%B0%81%E9%9D%A2%E5%9B%BE%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8Dv1.0_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L237)；[SRC035-S002｜三方案双语提示、逐维拆解和三层防护](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/01_%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%B0%81%E9%9D%A2%E5%9B%BE%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8Dv1.0_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L245)；[SRC035-S003｜选片、修订和下游接续](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/01_%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%B0%81%E9%9D%A2%E5%9B%BE%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8Dv1.0_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L280)。保留当前九阶段、三方案和选片修订；授权只要某封面时不强做全部资产。

- 明确要求十视角场景版：[SRC036-S001｜十视角版的阶段条件与复用范围](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/01_%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E8%A7%92%E8%89%B2%E5%9C%BA%E6%99%AF%E9%81%93%E5%85%B7%E6%8F%90%E5%8F%96_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L234)；[SRC036-S002｜十视角实际输出与交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/01_%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E8%A7%92%E8%89%B2%E5%9C%BA%E6%99%AF%E9%81%93%E5%85%B7%E6%8F%90%E5%8F%96_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L240)。十视角空间方案；不误认作十版角色三视图。

- [H032](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E6%9C%8D%E8%A3%85%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC012-S003｜H032服装一图提案板的条件分支与同款交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/01_%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E8%A7%92%E8%89%B2%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L648)：需服装A—D一图提案板；B平铺服装无人物，不把中文细节标签被通用无文字规则删掉。
- [H008](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E5%9C%BA%E6%99%AF%E7%8E%AF%E5%A2%83%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC003-S009｜H008场景100项方法与固定、状态、可见层](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/01_%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%9C%BA%E6%99%AF%E7%8E%AF%E5%A2%83%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L740)：需场景100项方法；固定空间、当次状态与机位可见层分开。
- [H033](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E6%9E%84%E5%9B%BE%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%9364%E6%9D%A1_%E5%85%89%E7%BA%BF%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1%E5%BA%9328%E6%9D%A1_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC008-S009｜H033构图64与布光28的共同调用及版本边界](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/01_%E5%9B%BE%E7%89%87%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E6%9E%84%E5%9B%BE%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L522)：需构图与布光联合；主体面积不等于身高占比，光源功率不等于照度。

**必要辅助候选**

- [L14-T0001｜脸型（Face Shape）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/14_%E8%A7%92%E8%89%B2%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L561)：脸型身份锁
- [L02-T0002｜远景（Long Shot）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/02_%E6%9E%84%E5%9B%BE%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L477)：远景空间取景
- [L04-T0002｜柔光（Soft Light）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/04_%E5%85%89%E7%BA%BF%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L244)：柔光可见细节
- [L52-T0003｜服装一致性](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/52_AI%E4%B8%80%E8%87%B4%E6%80%A7%E6%8E%A7%E5%88%B6%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L409)：跨图服装一致性

- 具体交付：咨询交付视觉方向与参考分工；授权后交所选角色/场景/道具卡、独立图片提示词或实际图像；记录设计稿与实际可用资产状态。
- 上游输入：接故事实体ID、阶段服装/伤湿、场景坐标、道具持有/数量、参考文件真实版本、画幅与原文字要求。
- 下游交接：给storyboard/video/advanced传CHAR/SC/PROP/REF及当前版本、脸/衣/空间/光色通道职责、固定锚点与起始状态；未生成/未验收参考不能写成已上传或已锁定。

### 分镜、导演与POV（storyboard）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/05_分镜、导演与POV.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/05_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E3%80%81%E5%AF%BC%E6%BC%94%E4%B8%8EPOV.md#L52)。
- 选择问题：剧本或小说转镜头、导演调度、POV设计，或继续/修改已完成部分分镜。

**当前主流程**

- 已有剧本转分镜：[SRC032-S001｜启动检查的实际输入分类和补全](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E6%8A%8A%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E8%BD%AC%E6%88%90%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L1010)；[SRC032-S002｜完整独立镜头和中文约束](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E6%8A%8A%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E8%BD%AC%E6%88%90%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L1018)；[SRC032-S003｜连续性检查和下游交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E6%8A%8A%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E8%BD%AC%E6%88%90%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L1028)。依据本次动作对白确定镜头时长，当前5秒例不是全任务强制默认。
- 小说逐句转分镜与首帧/双模式：[SRC043-S001｜输入、拆镜与原规则的采用条件](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E5%89%A7%E6%83%85%E8%BD%AC%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8Dv1.0_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L459)；[SRC043-S002｜三镜逐镜首帧与双模式完整输出](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E5%89%A7%E6%83%85%E8%BD%AC%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8Dv1.0_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L467)；[SRC043-S003｜原A—L清单的实际检查及交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E5%89%A7%E6%83%85%E8%BD%AC%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8Dv1.0_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L542)。保逐句覆盖；首帧静态、自然语言生成与精确参数档案职责分别保留。
- 人物第一人称视点：[SRC051-S001｜十五步、三套校验与十一补丁的采用说明](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/POV%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E4%BA%BA%E7%A7%B0%E6%A8%A1%E6%8B%9F%E7%9C%9F%E5%AE%9E%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A7%86%E8%A7%92%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L1176)；[SRC051-S002｜空间、十物与身份输入的实际登记](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/POV%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E4%BA%BA%E7%A7%B0%E6%A8%A1%E6%8B%9F%E7%9C%9F%E5%AE%9E%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A7%86%E8%A7%92%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L1184)；[SRC051-S003｜第一段独立拿杯检查的逐时点输出](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/POV%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E4%BA%BA%E7%A7%B0%E6%A8%A1%E6%8B%9F%E7%9C%9F%E5%AE%9E%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A7%86%E8%A7%92%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L1205)；[SRC051-S004｜第二段独立归杯回看的逐时点输出](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/POV%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E4%BA%BA%E7%A7%B0%E6%A8%A1%E6%8B%9F%E7%9C%9F%E5%AE%9E%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A7%86%E8%A7%92%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L1399)；[SRC051-S005｜三层约束、跨批交接和模型边界](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/POV%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E4%BA%BA%E7%A7%B0%E6%A8%A1%E6%8B%9F%E7%9C%9F%E5%AE%9E%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A7%86%E8%A7%92%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L1593)。使用当前较完整15步骤、校验与七块交付；人眼运动和摄影装置运动分别判断。

**条件历史版**

- [H019](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E8%BD%AC%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC032-S004｜H019十秒生成单元版的独立采用条件](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E6%8A%8A%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E8%BD%AC%E6%88%90%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L1034)：需要完整10秒单元六步模板；严格10秒与9.5—10弹性分别登记，不能覆盖用户已指定5秒。
- [H031](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E5%BD%B1%E8%A7%86%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E8%BD%ACAI%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC032-S005｜H031三十二模块启动判断与分镜生产接续](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E6%8A%8A%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E8%BD%AC%E6%88%90%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L1044)：需32模块素材分类/缺项判断；四来源、启动A—E与输出A/B不混淆，局部任务无核心阻塞即可制作。
- [H026](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E5%89%A7%E6%83%85%E8%BD%AC%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC_%E5%8F%8C%E6%A8%A1%E5%BC%8F%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC043-S005｜H026原版十五节双模式与数值档案的完整采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E5%89%A7%E6%83%85%E8%BD%AC%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8Dv1.0_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L560)：需要原版15节双模式；每镜首帧+模式一+模式二三块，数值档案不混入模式一，旧血迹不清零。
- [H028](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E8%BD%AC%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC043-S004｜H028更新版的时间弹性与特殊叙事采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%B0%8F%E8%AF%B4%E5%89%A7%E6%83%85%E8%BD%AC%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8Dv1.0_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L548)：需要更新10秒融合1—5子镜头、0.1秒时码；启用填充条款时合法9.5—10秒，非8秒。
- [H015](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/POV%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E4%BA%BA%E7%A7%B0%E6%A8%A1%E6%8B%9F%E7%9C%9F%E5%AE%9E%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A7%86%E8%A7%92%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC051-S006｜H015十四项POV补充的条件选择与当前完整交付](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/POV%E7%AC%AC%E4%B8%80%E4%BA%BA%E7%A7%B0%E6%A8%A1%E6%8B%9F%E7%9C%9F%E5%AE%9E%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A7%86%E8%A7%92%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L1605)：需POV补充；镜中脸按反射几何，眼高随坐蹲，全静音与仅外界静音分开。
- [H023](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E5%9C%BA%E9%9D%A2%E8%B0%83%E5%BA%A6%E5%BA%93_%E5%8F%99%E4%BA%8B%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%BA%93_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC021-S003｜H023双库二十调度六十叙事方法的条件整合](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%8F%99%E4%BA%8B%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L509)：场面调度与叙事信息互相影响时

- [L01-T0002｜缓慢推入（Slow Push-in）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/01_%E8%BF%90%E9%95%9C%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L825)：缓慢推入叙事动机
- [L06-T0003｜纵深场面调度（Depth Blocking）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/06_%E5%9C%BA%E9%9D%A2%E8%B0%83%E5%BA%A6%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L219)：纵深人物调度
- [L37-T0002｜主观视点](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/37_%E9%95%9C%E5%A4%B4%E8%A7%86%E7%82%B9%E4%B8%8E%E4%B8%BB%E8%A7%82%E6%80%A7%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L212)：主观视点边界
- [L08-T0001｜声画同步（Sound-Image Synchronization）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/08_%E5%A3%B0%E7%94%BB%E5%85%B3%E7%B3%BB%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L193)：声画同步

- 具体交付：咨询交付镜头结构比较；授权后交指定分镜、独立首帧/生成块、实际子镜计数、对白音效清单及逐镜末态。
- 上游输入：接SC/B/P/D/V覆盖、角色知识与时层、资产职责、空间轴线、眼位、道具归属、前镜声尾和用户选择的版本。
- 下游交接：给video传SHOT/GEN ID、实际时长、独立完整人物/场景/动作/声音和参考版本；给post传全片起点与局部时钟映射。跨批次从下一未覆盖原句及最近有效末态开始，不重演交接完成的动作。

### 视频生成提示词与一致性（video）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/06_视频生成提示词与一致性.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/06_%E8%A7%86%E9%A2%91%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E4%B8%8E%E4%B8%80%E8%87%B4%E6%80%A7.md#L46)。
- 选择问题：把已选分镜变成可复制生成提示词，处理多参考/身份连续、局部优化或漫剧生成结构。

**当前主流程**

- 分镜到模式A档案/模式B镜头描述：[SRC047-S001｜平台边界、宏观锚点与矛盾采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/04_%E8%A7%86%E9%A2%91%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L519)；[SRC047-S002｜角色档案及模式A实际输出](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/04_%E8%A7%86%E9%A2%91%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L525)；[SRC047-S003｜模式B两镜六段完整描述与独立负条件](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/04_%E8%A7%86%E9%A2%91%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L542)；[SRC047-S004｜字段覆盖和实际交接检查](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/04_%E8%A7%86%E9%A2%91%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L567)。单元自包含，固定身份与当次状态分开；实际平台能力未知用通用稿。
- 微调已有镜头：[SRC041-S001｜原分镜诊断、参数与采用理由](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%BE%AE%E8%B0%83%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L335)；[SRC041-S002｜自然语言优化版与八层约束](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%BE%AE%E8%B0%83%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L343)；[SRC041-S003｜四个旧模型名称的文本映射与英文实际输出](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%BE%AE%E8%B0%83%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L353)；[SRC041-S004｜四阶段、输出六项与自检交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%BE%AE%E8%B0%83%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L375)。先诊断再改目标镜，自然语言、技术档案与条件负向分别交付。

**条件历史版**

- [H004](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/AI%E6%BC%AB%E5%89%A7%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC047-S005｜H004漫剧双模式、条件模块与三层时钟](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/04_%E8%A7%86%E9%A2%91%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L573)：需漫剧双模式、六层或4—12秒分段；子镜、动作阶段、生成单元三种计数分开。
- [H005](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/AI%E6%BC%AB%E5%89%A7%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E6%A8%A1%E6%9D%BF%E5%8F%82%E8%80%83_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC047-S006｜H005二十三部分单镜模板与双模式流程的选择](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/04_%E8%A7%86%E9%A2%91%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L585)：明确需23部分单镜模板；与双模式择版，Pavo未知不推定厂商或接口。
- [H017](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E4%BC%98%E5%8C%96%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC041-S005｜H017十五项优化补充与单镜连续、条件反提示](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%BE%AE%E8%B0%83%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L381)：需15种优化方法、12表演通道/情绪曲线及八反提示层；单镜可推近，不用无关负向封死所需运动。

**必要辅助候选**

- [L51-T0001｜主体描述](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/51_AI%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E5%B7%A5%E7%A8%8B%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L382)：主体必要信息
- [L52-T0001｜面部一致性](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/52_AI%E4%B8%80%E8%87%B4%E6%80%A7%E6%8E%A7%E5%88%B6%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L383)：面部身份连续
- [L53-T0001｜姿态控制](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/53_ControlNet%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%82%E8%80%83%E5%9B%BE%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L383)：实际支持的姿态参考职责
- [L17-T0003｜手指错误（Finger Errors）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/17_AI%E5%8F%8D%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L525)：手部失败的针对性负向

- 具体交付：咨询交付单元拆分、参考职责与修复优先级；授权后交每单元完整正向、适用负向、音频/后期字段及参数档案。
- 上游输入：继承当前有效SHOT/GEN版本、动作关键路径、角色阶段、空间/手物/光声起终、参考文件与实际平台已核能力。
- 下游交接：给生成执行或post传GEN/TAKE版本、目标状态与实际结果分别记录、有效/失败证据、修复对象、声画时间映射；种子不作身份保证，未生成不能记成已验证或READY成片。

### 表演、微表情与群体动作（performance）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/07_表演、微表情与群体动作.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/07_%E8%A1%A8%E6%BC%94%E3%80%81%E5%BE%AE%E8%A1%A8%E6%83%85%E4%B8%8E%E7%BE%A4%E4%BD%93%E5%8A%A8%E4%BD%9C.md#L45)。
- 选择问题：人物表演、眼神、微表情、身体动作或群体调度需要具体化，与对白/景别/时间协调。

**当前主流程**

- 身体动作与支撑/握物：[SRC016-S001｜全字段版本差异与已有成果采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/05_%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A1%A8%E6%BC%94%E4%B8%8E%E5%8A%A8%E4%BD%9C/%E8%BA%AB%E4%BD%93%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L674)；[SRC016-S002｜完整采用例及上下游交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/05_%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A1%A8%E6%BC%94%E4%B8%8E%E5%8A%A8%E4%BD%9C/%E8%BA%AB%E4%BD%93%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L787)。按当前动作目标选体态，身体静态与跑跳惯性分开。
- 眼部动作与多人物眼线：[SRC026-S001｜全字段版本差异与已有成果采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/05_%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A1%A8%E6%BC%94%E4%B8%8E%E5%8A%A8%E4%BD%9C/12%E3%80%81%E7%9C%BC%E7%9D%9B%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L710)；[SRC026-S002｜完整采用例及上下游交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/05_%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A1%A8%E6%BC%94%E4%B8%8E%E5%8A%A8%E4%BD%9C/12%E3%80%81%E7%9C%BC%E7%9D%9B%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L828)。眼球、头、会聚、摄影焦点分开。
- 表情/微表情段落：[SRC020-S001｜全字段版本差异与已有成果采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/05_%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A1%A8%E6%BC%94%E4%B8%8E%E5%8A%A8%E4%BD%9C/%E5%BE%AE%E8%A1%A8%E6%83%85%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L718)；[SRC020-S002｜完整采用例及上下游交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/05_%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A1%A8%E6%BC%94%E4%B8%8E%E5%8A%A8%E4%BD%9C/%E5%BE%AE%E8%A1%A8%E6%83%85%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L837)。按角色各自目标与景别设计，不用表情推断唯一真实心理。
- 人群分层动作：[SRC029-S001｜全字段版本差异与已有成果采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/05_%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A1%A8%E6%BC%94%E4%B8%8E%E5%8A%A8%E4%BD%9C/25%E3%80%81%E7%BE%A4%E4%BD%93%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E4%B8%8E%E4%BA%BA%E7%BE%A4%E8%B0%83%E5%BA%A6%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L430)；[SRC029-S002｜完整采用例及上下游交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/05_%E4%BA%BA%E7%89%A9%E8%A1%A8%E6%BC%94%E4%B8%8E%E5%8A%A8%E4%BD%9C/25%E3%80%81%E7%BE%A4%E4%BD%93%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E4%B8%8E%E4%BA%BA%E7%BE%A4%E8%B0%83%E5%BA%A6%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L503)。人数、有效面积、路径与结束分布明确。
- 嵌回分镜：[SRC041-S001｜原分镜诊断、参数与采用理由](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%BE%AE%E8%B0%83%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L335)；[SRC041-S002｜自然语言优化版与八层约束](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%BE%AE%E8%B0%83%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L343)；[SRC041-S004｜四阶段、输出六项与自检交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%BE%AE%E8%B0%83%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L375)。表演、口型、对白与拟音用同一时间轴。

**必要辅助候选**

- [L12-T0001｜直视（Direct Gaze）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/12_%E7%9C%BC%E7%9D%9B%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L721)：直视的对象/持续时间

- [L25-T0003｜人群流动（Crowd Flow）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/25_%E7%BE%A4%E4%BD%93%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E4%B8%8E%E4%BA%BA%E7%BE%A4%E8%B0%83%E5%BA%A6%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L467)：人群流动的路线与密度

- 具体交付：咨询交付强度/节奏和可见性选择；授权后交触发—变化—维持—释放的物理表演序列，群体人数和独立人物响应。
- 上游输入：接人物目标/知识、伤损疲劳、前镜姿态/视线/呼吸相位、持有物、对白原句、镜头景别与占时。
- 下游交接：给storyboard/video/sound传人物分别编号的动作/口型/视线/声源时间、结束姿势、支撑脚、手物、泪痕和群体分布；近景细节不强塞远景，动作延续不重置伤湿。

### 打斗、仙侠与特效（effects）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/08_打斗、仙侠与特效.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/08_%E6%89%93%E6%96%97%E3%80%81%E4%BB%99%E4%BE%A0%E4%B8%8E%E7%89%B9%E6%95%88.md#L47)。
- 选择问题：设计打斗攻防、仙侠手诀法术、能量或环境特效，并保持接触因果与后果。

**当前主流程**

- 武学攻防：[SRC042-S001｜全字段版本差异与已有成果采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/08_%E7%89%B9%E6%95%88%E4%B8%8E%E4%BB%99%E4%BE%A0%E6%89%93%E6%96%97/%E6%AD%A6%E5%AD%A6%E6%89%93%E6%96%97%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L722)；[SRC042-S002｜完整采用例及上下游交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/08_%E7%89%B9%E6%95%88%E4%B8%8E%E4%BB%99%E4%BE%A0%E6%89%93%E6%96%97/%E6%AD%A6%E5%AD%A6%E6%89%93%E6%96%97%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L843)。先距离—发力—接触—反馈—收束，风格服务事件结果。

- 具体交付：咨询交付真实/幻想风格、攻防事件链和制作风险；授权后交完整动作/手诀/特效/声同步段落及损伤、资源余额。
- 上游输入：接双方身份、站位距离、武器归属、手指占用、伤损、既定法则、可用资源、胜负与不可改结果。
- 下游交接：给storyboard/video/sound传接触时点、受力/传播路径、身体和粒子各自时钟、武器持有人、残留痕迹、资源消耗、时停范围与解除位置；死者/冻结对象不能被通用动态规则复活。

### 声音设计、对白与声画关系（sound）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/09_声音设计、对白与声画关系.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/09_%E5%A3%B0%E9%9F%B3%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1%E3%80%81%E5%AF%B9%E7%99%BD%E4%B8%8E%E5%A3%B0%E7%94%BB%E5%85%B3%E7%B3%BB.md#L44)。
- 选择问题：声音设计、对白/配音、拟音、环境声、声画关系或已有音轨诊断。

**当前主流程**

- 环境/事件声设计：[SRC018-S001｜全字段版本差异与已有成果采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/06_%E5%A3%B0%E9%9F%B3%E4%B8%8E%E9%9F%B3%E4%B9%90/%E5%A3%B0%E9%9F%B3%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L551)；[SRC018-S002｜完整采用例及上下游交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/06_%E5%A3%B0%E9%9F%B3%E4%B8%8E%E9%9F%B3%E4%B9%90/%E5%A3%B0%E9%9F%B3%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L644)。按声源位置、接触事件和声音视点，不凭没听过的素材做实测诊断。
- 声画同步/对位/先入延续：[SRC017-S001｜全字段版本差异与已有成果采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/07_%E5%89%AA%E8%BE%91%E4%B8%8E%E5%90%8E%E6%9C%9F/%E5%A3%B0%E7%94%BB%E5%85%B3%E7%B3%BB%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L180)；[SRC017-S002｜完整采用例及上下游交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/07_%E5%89%AA%E8%BE%91%E4%B8%8E%E5%90%8E%E6%9C%9F/%E5%A3%B0%E7%94%BB%E5%85%B3%E7%B3%BB%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L213)。声音与画面共享事件ID，切画面不自动切断声音。
- 对白话轮与逐字声音清单：[SRC030-S001｜70条独立稿的差异保留与补齐说明](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/27%E3%80%81%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%B0%E8%AF%8D%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L494)；[SRC030-S002｜实际留言与纸面全文，避免空壳交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/27%E3%80%81%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%B0%E8%AF%8D%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L500)；[SRC030-S003｜留言时间、停顿和录制结束实际状态](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/27%E3%80%81%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%B0%E8%AF%8D%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L506)；[SRC030-S004｜完整留言制作文本与旁白备选的知识差](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/27%E3%80%81%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%B0%E8%AF%8D%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L512)；[SRC030-S005｜原沉默、重叠和重复数值保留与不可误用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/27%E3%80%81%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%B0%E8%AF%8D%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L520)；[SRC030-S006｜文本复核、版本选择和下一事件输入](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/27%E3%80%81%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%B0%E8%AF%8D%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L526)。保原句、媒介与知情范围；清单从正文派生，不反向凭空加台词。

**条件历史版**

- [H010](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E8%A7%86%E9%A2%91%E5%A3%B0%E9%9F%B3%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC018-S003｜H010八十声法、单位与三种延迟的条件整合](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/06_%E5%A3%B0%E9%9F%B3%E4%B8%8E%E9%9F%B3%E4%B9%90/%E5%A3%B0%E9%9F%B3%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L650)：需80声法与完整声音模板；制作延迟、物理传播、叙事错位三种延迟分开，静音/半速单位核对
- [H024](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%B0%E8%AF%8D%E6%8A%80%E5%B7%A7%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC030-S007｜H024七十对白方法与话轮、知识、媒介状态](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/02_%E5%89%A7%E6%9C%AC%E4%B8%8E%E5%A4%A7%E7%BA%B2%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/27%E3%80%81%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%B0%E8%AF%8D%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L533)：需70对白方法和媒介状态；录制/保存/发送/播放/听懂分别记，沉默不等于同意

**必要辅助候选**

- [L08-T0001｜声画同步（Sound-Image Synchronization）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/08_%E5%A3%B0%E7%94%BB%E5%85%B3%E7%B3%BB%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L193)：同步接触事件

- [L45-T0001｜木地板](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/45_%E6%8B%9F%E9%9F%B3%E4%B8%8E%E9%9F%B3%E6%95%88%E5%88%86%E7%B1%BB%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L324)：木地板接触拟音

- 具体交付：咨询交付声源/空间与声画方案；授权后交逐事件声音提示、逐字配音清单、轨道/时间/混响/尾音及选择的混音设计。
- 上游输入：接画面事件、D/V原句、发声者与听者、声源方位、场景材质、人物知识、音频可访问范围和全片时间映射。
- 下游交接：给music传对白占用窗口/叙事音乐功能；给video/post传AUDIO/声轨ID、局部/全片起止、尾音跨切、静音类型与媒介知情末态。设计分贝不当测量值，未听音轨不称已诊断。

### 音乐、BGM与歌曲（music）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/10_音乐、BGM与歌曲.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/10_%E9%9F%B3%E4%B9%90%E3%80%81BGM%E4%B8%8E%E6%AD%8C%E6%9B%B2.md#L43)。
- 选择问题：选择或创作BGM、纯器乐、无词人声、歌曲、歌词/编曲，或按已有音乐反馈修改。

**当前主流程**

- %90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L180)；[SRC049-S006｜版本确认与后续试听](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/06_%E5%A3%B0%E9%9F%B3%E4%B8%8E%E9%9F%B3%E4%B9%90/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90BGM%E9%9F%B3%E4%B9%90%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L192)。先用途与模式A/B，再小节蓝图和平台字段；无歌词不自动等于无人声。

- 具体交付：咨询交付音乐DNA和必要风格备选；授权后交完整指定歌词、和声/编曲蓝图或独立音乐生成字段，标明设计时长与实际试听状态。
- 上游输入：接已选主题/风格/语言、人声模式、原歌词、音域/音色锁、剧情/对白窗口、音乐动机版本、实际可听素材。
- 下游交接：给sound/post传MUSIC/版本、BPM/拍号/小节到秒的映射、进入/退出/循环接点、歌词原句与唱词时窗、尾音；降调/改词/改速各自传播，不只改数字而保留互相冲突音高。

### 电商、品牌与商业广告（commerce）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/11_电商、品牌与商业广告.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/11_%E7%94%B5%E5%95%86%E3%80%81%E5%93%81%E7%89%8C%E4%B8%8E%E5%95%86%E4%B8%9A%E5%B9%BF%E5%91%8A.md#L49)。
- 选择问题：商品主图/详情、带货视频或品牌广告创意、制作包与多时长/画幅版本。

**当前主流程**

- 商品全套静态图：[SRC034-S001｜四阶段与二十项确认的实际采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/09_%E7%94%B5%E5%95%86%E4%B8%8E%E5%95%86%E4%B8%9A%E5%B9%BF%E5%91%8A/%E7%94%B5%E5%95%86%E5%B9%B3%E5%8F%B0%E5%95%86%E5%93%81%E5%85%A8%E5%A5%97%E5%9B%BE%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L220)；[SRC034-S002｜五主图八详情的完整独立提示词](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/09_%E7%94%B5%E5%95%86%E4%B8%8E%E5%95%86%E4%B8%9A%E5%B9%BF%E5%91%8A/%E7%94%B5%E5%95%86%E5%B9%B3%E5%8F%B0%E5%95%86%E5%93%81%E5%85%A8%E5%A5%97%E5%9B%BE%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L226)；[SRC034-S003｜七项自审与可执行交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/09_%E7%94%B5%E5%95%86%E4%B8%8E%E5%95%86%E4%B8%9A%E5%B9%BF%E5%91%8A/%E7%94%B5%E5%95%86%E5%B9%B3%E5%8F%B0%E5%95%86%E5%93%81%E5%85%A8%E5%A5%97%E5%9B%BE%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L295)。真实产品资料→图片结构→独立主图/详情提示→核对；用户指定数量优先，已有确认不重复问20项。

- 具体交付：咨询交付创意主张、真实卖点证据与所选形式；授权后交指定套图/广告脚本/独立生成块或生产包，分别标策略就绪、素材就绪、成片验收。
- 上游输入：接产品型号/结构/尺寸/包装原字、真实证据、品牌锁、渠道/受众、已选创意、已核素材与本次交付；未知价格/认证不编造。
- 下游交接：给images/video/sound/post传PRODUCT/BRAND/ASSET和版本、文案原句、卖点证据、画幅重排、GEN与全片时钟、素材门及失败对象；发布/付费不是此咨询入口自动动作。

### 剪辑、修复与成片复盘（post）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/12_剪辑、修复与成片复盘.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/12_%E5%89%AA%E8%BE%91%E3%80%81%E4%BF%AE%E5%A4%8D%E4%B8%8E%E6%88%90%E7%89%87%E5%A4%8D%E7%9B%98.md#L45)。
- 选择问题：已有素材需要剪辑、局部修复、声画调整或成片问题复盘。

**当前主流程**

- 根据实际素材组接：[SRC011-S001｜独立稿与两合集的采用及逐条覆盖](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/07_%E5%89%AA%E8%BE%91%E4%B8%8E%E5%90%8E%E6%9C%9F/%E5%89%AA%E8%BE%91%E4%B8%8E%E8%92%99%E5%A4%AA%E5%A5%87%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L180)；[SRC011-S002｜实际素材决策、完整剪辑交付与复用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/07_%E5%89%AA%E8%BE%91%E4%B8%8E%E5%90%8E%E6%9C%9F/%E5%89%AA%E8%BE%91%E4%B8%8E%E8%92%99%E5%A4%AA%E5%A5%87%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L211)。先实际片段证据与切点，再完整时间线；不虚构独立剪辑总模板。
- 需回到镜头提示词定点修复：[SRC041-S001｜原分镜诊断、参数与采用理由](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%BE%AE%E8%B0%83%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L335)；[SRC041-S004｜四阶段、输出六项与自检交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%BE%AE%E8%B0%83%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L375)。仅修实际失败对象，保留有效部分和已锁原句。
- 声轨跨切/对位：[SRC017-S001｜全字段版本差异与已有成果采用](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/07_%E5%89%AA%E8%BE%91%E4%B8%8E%E5%90%8E%E6%9C%9F/%E5%A3%B0%E7%94%BB%E5%85%B3%E7%B3%BB%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L180)；[SRC017-S002｜完整采用例及上下游交接](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/07_%E5%89%AA%E8%BE%91%E4%B8%8E%E5%90%8E%E6%9C%9F/%E5%A3%B0%E7%94%BB%E5%85%B3%E7%B3%BB%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L213)。声尾与可听对白从原时间轴传入，不因画面替换就重新发送故事信息。

**条件历史版**

- [H017](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/%E4%BC%98%E5%8C%96%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC041-S005｜H017十五项优化补充与单镜连续、条件反提示](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E5%BE%AE%E8%B0%83%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E7%9A%84%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L381)：重生成或提示词修复需要15种优化方法时；无需全套重做
- [H010](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/92_%E5%8E%86%E5%8F%B2%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/AI%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E8%A7%86%E9%A2%91%E5%A3%B0%E9%9F%B3%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D_%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E7%89%88.md) → [SRC018-S003｜H010八十声法、单位与三种延迟的条件整合](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/06_%E5%A3%B0%E9%9F%B3%E4%B8%8E%E9%9F%B3%E4%B9%90/%E5%A3%B0%E9%9F%B3%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L650)：声画错位涉及三种延迟/静音时

**必要辅助候选**

- [L07-T0003｜匹配剪辑（Match Cut）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/07_%E5%89%AA%E8%BE%91%E4%B8%8E%E8%92%99%E5%A4%AA%E5%A5%87%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L220)：匹配剪辑
- [L41-T0002｜动作匹配](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/41_%E5%8C%B9%E9%85%8D%E5%89%AA%E8%BE%91%E4%B8%8E%E8%BD%AC%E5%9C%BA%E8%AF%AD%E6%B3%95%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L227)：动作匹配的前后相位
- [L44-T0001｜音量平衡](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/44_%E6%B7%B7%E9%9F%B3%E4%B8%8E%E7%A9%BA%E9%97%B4%E5%A3%B0%E5%AD%A6%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L382)：对白与声音平衡
- [L55-T0001｜去噪](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/55_AI%E5%90%8E%E6%9C%9F%E4%B8%8E%E4%BF%AE%E5%A4%8D%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L381)：已确诊噪声问题时去噪
- [L55-T0002｜去模糊](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/55_AI%E5%90%8E%E6%9C%9F%E4%B8%8E%E4%BF%AE%E5%A4%8D%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L394)：模糊问题时评估修复，不能称已还原未拍细节

- 具体交付：咨询交付有时间点的问题/收益损失比较；授权后交时间线、修复提示或编辑结果及实际有效/失效证据。
- 上游输入：接真实CLIP/TAKE/AUDIO版本与访问范围、时间基准/帧率、锁定对白、原句覆盖、起终状态与必须保留内容。
- 下游交接：返交对应video/images/sound模块的定点补料任务；向当前作品项目交EDL/导出版本、素材入出点、变速映射、全片声画时间、状态连续检查及失败断点，未做导出不称成片完成。

### 3D、虚拟制片与交互创作（advanced）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/13_3D、虚拟制片与交互创作.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/13_3D%E3%80%81%E8%99%9A%E6%8B%9F%E5%88%B6%E7%89%87%E4%B8%8E%E4%BA%A4%E4%BA%92%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C.md#L47)。
- 选择问题：需要3D资产/空间重建、虚拟制片、实时渲染、交互分支或沉浸路线比较。

**当前主流程**

- 纯AI/三维/实时/交互路线选择：[L57-T0001｜建模](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/57_3D%E4%B8%8E%E7%A9%BA%E9%97%B4%E9%87%8D%E5%BB%BA%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L383)；[L56-T0002｜实时渲染](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/56_%E8%99%9A%E6%8B%9F%E5%88%B6%E7%89%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%AE%9E%E6%97%B6%E6%B8%B2%E6%9F%93%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L395)；[L56-T0003｜虚拟摄像机](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/56_%E8%99%9A%E6%8B%9F%E5%88%B6%E7%89%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%AE%9E%E6%97%B6%E6%B8%B2%E6%9F%93%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L408)；[L58-T0001｜分支叙事](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/58_%E4%BA%A4%E4%BA%92%E5%8F%99%E4%BA%8B%E4%B8%8E%E6%B2%89%E6%B5%B8%E5%AA%92%E4%BD%93%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L382)；[L58-T0002｜选择](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/58_%E4%BA%A4%E4%BA%92%E5%8F%99%E4%BA%8B%E4%B8%8E%E6%B2%89%E6%B5%B8%E5%AA%92%E4%BD%93%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L395)；[L58-T0003｜后果](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/58_%E4%BA%A4%E4%BA%92%E5%8F%99%E4%BA%8B%E4%B8%8E%E6%B2%89%E6%B5%B8%E5%AA%92%E4%BD%93%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L408)。当前无独立三维总流程TXT；用这些实际词条与已有模块组合，不虚构已验收三维模板、已建模型或已运行引擎。
- **必要辅助候选**

- [L57-T0002｜材质](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/57_3D%E4%B8%8E%E7%A9%BA%E9%97%B4%E9%87%8D%E5%BB%BA%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L396)：建模材质与图片材质职责区别

- [L52-T0003｜服装一致性](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/52_AI%E4%B8%80%E8%87%B4%E6%80%A7%E6%8E%A7%E5%88%B6%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L409)：不同路线服装身份连续

- 具体交付：咨询交付路线收益/条件、资产空间要求与可验证节点；授权后交指定3D/交互设计或实际实现，按工具实际能力标证据，不自动安装软件。
- 上游输入：接目标体验、资产实文件/单位/坐标系、已确认地图与身份、工具实际版本/能力、允许实施范围、分支前状态。
- 下游交接：给实现/实时/交互或images/video传WORLD/ASSET/NODE/BRANCH ID、尺度/坐标/几何推断范围、进入条件—选择—状态变化—结果—合流守卫；分支合流不得把不同道具/知识末态直接混成同一状态，未重建隐藏面保持未知。

### 素材解读与提示词反推（analysis）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/14_素材解读与提示词反推.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/14_%E7%B4%A0%E6%9D%90%E8%A7%A3%E8%AF%BB%E4%B8%8E%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E5%8F%8D%E6%8E%A8.md#L51)。
- 选择问题：解读实际图片/视频/音频、反推提示词，或从参考中借用部分表达并原创改造。

**当前主流程**

- 跨模态或需保持原文字的反推：[SRC024-S003｜H021全模态反推：单代码框与必需内容优先](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/04_%E8%A7%86%E9%A2%91%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D/AI%E5%8F%8D%E9%A2%98%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L567)。采用H021当前条件层，先核模态访问；观察、推断、设计分别登记，反推不等于找回真实原提示。
- 视频逐镜与首帧/运动分离：[SRC023-S003｜H022视频反推：观察证据、首帧与复现运动分离](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/01_%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E4%B8%8E%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84/03_%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C/%E8%BF%90%E9%95%9C%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L949)。采用H022当前条件层，读真实运动/切镜证据；单帧不能证明速度或动作。

**条件历史版**

**必要辅助候选**

- [L03-T0002｜标准镜头（Standard Lens）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/03_%E5%85%89%E5%AD%A6%E4%B8%8E%E9%95%9C%E5%A4%B4%E7%89%B9%E6%80%A7%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L197)：标准镜头仅作推定/设计，不说实测焦距

- [L08-T0001｜声画同步（Sound-Image Synchronization）](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/08_%E5%A3%B0%E7%94%BB%E5%85%B3%E7%B3%BB%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L193)：实际可听音轨与画面同步
- [L51-T0001｜主体描述](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/02_%E8%AF%AD%E8%A8%80%E5%BA%93%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99/51_AI%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E5%B7%A5%E7%A8%8B%E5%BA%93_%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%87%E4%B8%8E%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99.md#L382)：将观察转为必要主体描述

- 具体交付：咨询交付证据特征及可借用表达；授权后交完整独立反推提示或改造方案，标来源、访问范围、估计值与新增设计。
- 上游输入：接实际文件/版本、可访问帧段与音轨、用户要保留/改变部分、原文字与已选复现目标；无声轨不能凭画面称已听到。
- 下游交接：给images/video/music/sound传OBS/帧或时间证据、REF职责、已选借用特征、推断置信范围、原创改动与待核参数；参考来源不能自动变成项目已确认事实或授权发布。

### 提示词框架扩写与知识讲解（framework）

- 入口：[91_创作小秘工作库/03_咨询模块/15_提示词框架扩写与知识讲解.md](../skills/film-creation-library/references/knowledge_base/91_%E5%88%9B%E4%BD%9C%E5%B0%8F%E7%A7%98%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E5%BA%93/03_%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97/15_%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D%E6%A1%86%E6%9E%B6%E6%89%A9%E5%86%99%E4%B8%8E%E7%9F%A5%E8%AF%86%E8%AE%B2%E8%A7%A3.md#L40)。
- 选择问题：扩写已有提示词框架、解释库内方法、排查规则冲突或改进咨询调用方式。
